+ library(dplyr)

+ # Fit the interaction model used in the second pass
+ interact_fit <- lm(
+   performance_decrease_under_thermal_and_Air ~
+     Sensitivity * Age +
+     performance_decrease_under_noise_and_light * Age,
+   data = data_SCFs
+ )
+ 
+ cat("=== Interaction model summary ===\n")
+ summary(interact_fit)
+ 
+ # Build a prediction grid: vary Sensitivity across its range, for each Age group
+ sens_range <- seq(
+   min(data_SCFs$Sensitivity, na.rm = TRUE),
+   max(data_SCFs$Sensitivity, na.rm = TRUE),
+   length.out = 100
+ )
+ 
+ age_levels <- unique(na.omit(data_SCFs$Age))
+ noise_mean  <- mean(data_SCFs$performance_decrease_under_noise_and_light, na.rm = TRUE)
+ 
+ pred_grid <- expand.grid(
+   Sensitivity = sens_range,
+   Age = age_levels
+ ) |>
+   mutate(performance_decrease_under_noise_and_light = noise_mean)
+ 
+ pred_grid$fitted <- predict(interact_fit, newdata = pred_grid)
+ 
+ # Count observations per Age group for labelling
+ age_counts <- data_SCFs |>
+   filter(!is.na(Age)) |>
+   count(Age) |>
+   mutate(label = paste0(Age, " (n=", n, ")"))
+ 
+ pred_grid <- pred_grid |>
+   left_join(age_counts |> select(Age, label), by = "Age")
+ 
+ ggplot(pred_grid, aes(x = Sensitivity, y = fitted, colour = label)) +
+   geom_line(linewidth = 1.1) +
+   geom_rug(
+     data = data_SCFs |>
+       filter(!is.na(Age)) |>
+       left_join(age_counts |> select(Age, label), by = "Age"),
+     aes(x = Sensitivity, colour = label),

> # Re-initialise results containers
+ comprehensive_reg_results <- data.frame(
+   Outcome_Var = character(), Predictor_Vars = character(),
+   Demographic_Vars = character(), Model_Type = character(),
+   R_Squared = numeric(), Adjusted_R_Squared = numeric(),
+   Omega_Squared = numeric(), F_Statistic = numeric(),
+   P_Value = numeric(), N_Observations = integer(),
+   AIC = numeric(), BIC = numeric(),
+   stringsAsFactors = FALSE
+ )
+ all_regression_models <- list()
+ 
+ cat("Estimated total model fits:", length(numeric_vars) * choose(length(sig_predictors), 2) * 2, "\n\n")
Estimated total model fits: 36 
> for (outcome in numeric_vars) {
+   
+   predictor_candidates <- setdiff(sig_predictors, outcome)
+   
+   if (length(predictor_candidates) < 2) {
+     cat("Skipping outcome '", outcome, "': Insufficient sig. predictors after exclusion\n")
+     next
+   }
+   
+   predictor_combos <- combn(predictor_candidates, 2, simplify = FALSE)
+   
+   cat("\n", rep("=", 80), "\n", sep = "")
+   cat("OUTCOME: ", outcome, " | Predictor combos: ", length(predictor_combos), "\n")
+   cat(rep("=", 80), "\n", sep = "")
+   
+   for (pred_idx in seq_along(predictor_combos)) {
+     
+     predictors     <- predictor_combos[[pred_idx]]
+     predictor_label <- paste(predictors, collapse = " + ")
+     cat("\n  Predictors: ", predictor_label, "\n")
+     
+     all_demo_combos <- list(
+       character(0),
+       c("Age", "How_quickly_notice_IEQ_parameter_change")
+     )
+     
+     for (demo_idx in seq_along(all_demo_combos)) {
+       
+       demographics      <- all_demo_combos[[demo_idx]]
+       demographic_label <- if (length(demographics) == 0) "None" else paste(demographics, collapse = " + ")
+       
+       model_results <- tryCatch({
+         analy_SCFA$perform_comprehensive_regression_analysis(
+           outcome_var            = outcome,
+           predictors             = predictors,
+           demographic_predictors = if (length(demographics) > 0) demographics else NULL,
+           interaction_terms      = TRUE,
+           include_logistic       = FALSE
+         )
+       }, error = function(e) {
+         cat("  ⚠ Error (", demographic_label, "): ", e$message, "\n", sep = "")
+         return(NULL)
+       })
+       
+       if (!is.null(model_results) && !is.null(model_results$multiple)) {
+         
+         model_obj     <- model_results$multiple$model
+         model_summary <- summary(model_obj)
+         metrics       <- extract_model_metrics(model_obj, model_summary)
+         
+         if (!is.null(metrics)) {
+           omega_sq <- calculate_omega_squared(model_obj)
+           
+           results_row <- data.frame(
+             Outcome_Var        = outcome,
+             Predictor_Vars     = predictor_label,
+             Demographic_Vars   = demographic_label,
+             Model_Type         = "Multiple Linear Regression",
+             R_Squared          = metrics$r_squared,
+             Adjusted_R_Squared = metrics$adj_r_squared,
+             Omega_Squared      = omega_sq,
+             F_Statistic        = metrics$f_statistic,
+             P_Value            = metrics$p_value,
+             N_Observations     = metrics$n_observations,
+             AIC                = metrics$aic,
+             BIC                = metrics$bic,
+             stringsAsFactors   = FALSE
+           )
+           
+           comprehensive_reg_results <- rbind(comprehensive_reg_results, results_row)
+           
+           model_key <- paste(outcome, "|", predictor_label, "|", demographic_label, sep = "")
+           all_regression_models[[model_key]] <- list(
+             model = model_obj, summary = model_summary, metrics = metrics
+           )
+           
+           cat("  ✓ [", demographic_label, "] ω²=", round(omega_sq, 4),
+               " | R²=", round(metrics$r_squared, 4),
+               " | AdjR²=", round(metrics$adj_r_squared, 4),
+               " | n=", metrics$n_observations, "\n", sep = "")
+         }
+       }
+     }
+   }
+ }
+ 
+ cat("\n\n", rep("=", 80), "\n", sep = "")
+ cat("FINAL RESULTS — sorted by ω²\n")
+ cat(rep("=", 80), "\n\n", sep = "")
+ 
+ results_final <- comprehensive_reg_results[order(-comprehensive_reg_results$Omega_Squared), ]
+ print(results_final)
+ 
+ write.csv(results_final, "comprehensive_regression_results_FINAL.csv", row.names = FALSE, na = "")

> comprehensive_reg_results <- data.frame(
+   Outcome_Var = character(), Predictor_Vars = character(),
+   Demographic_Vars = character(), Model_Type = character(),
+   R_Squared = numeric(), Adjusted_R_Squared = numeric(),
+   Omega_Squared = numeric(), F_Statistic = numeric(),
+   P_Value = numeric(), N_Observations = integer(),
+   AIC = numeric(), BIC = numeric(),
+   stringsAsFactors = FALSE
+ )
+ all_regression_models <- list()
+ 
+ for (outcome in numeric_vars) {
+   
+   predictor_candidates <- setdiff(sig_predictors, outcome)
+   if (length(predictor_candidates) < 2) {
+     cat("Skipping '", outcome, "': insufficient sig. predictors\n"); next
+   }
+   predictor_combos <- combn(predictor_candidates, 2, simplify = FALSE)
+   
+   cat("\n", rep("=", 70), "\n", sep = "")
+   cat("OUTCOME: ", outcome, "\n")
+   cat(rep("=", 70), "\n", sep = "")
+   
+   for (pred_idx in seq_along(predictor_combos)) {
+     
+     predictors      <- predictor_combos[[pred_idx]]
+     predictor_label <- paste(predictors, collapse = " + ")
+     cat("  Predictors: ", predictor_label, "\n")
+     
+     all_demo_combos <- list(
+       character(0),
+       c("Age", "How_quickly_notice_IEQ_parameter_change")
+     )
+     
+     for (demo_idx in seq_along(all_demo_combos)) {
+       
+       demographics      <- all_demo_combos[[demo_idx]]
+       demographic_label <- if (length(demographics) == 0) "None"
+                            else paste(demographics, collapse = " + ")
+       
+       model_results <- tryCatch({
+         analy_SCFA$perform_comprehensive_regression_analysis(
+           outcome_var            = outcome,
+           predictors             = predictors,
+           demographic_predictors = if (length(demographics) > 0) demographics else NULL,
+           interaction_terms      = TRUE,
+           include_logistic       = FALSE
+         )
+       }, error = function(e) {
+         cat("  ⚠ Error [", demographic_label, "]: ", e$message, "\n", sep = ""); NULL
+       })
+       
+       if (!is.null(model_results) && !is.null(model_results$multiple)) {
+         
+         use_demo_model <- length(demographics) > 0 && !is.null(model_results$demographic)
+         model_obj      <- if (use_demo_model) model_results$demographic$model
+                           else                model_results$multiple$model
+         model_type     <- if (use_demo_model) "ANCOVA (demographics controlled)"
+                           else                "Multiple Linear Regression"
+         model_summary  <- summary(model_obj)
+         metrics        <- extract_model_metrics(model_obj, model_summary)
+         
+         if (!is.null(metrics)) {
+           omega_sq <- calculate_omega_squared(model_obj)
+           
+           comprehensive_reg_results <- rbind(comprehensive_reg_results, data.frame(
+             Outcome_Var        = outcome,
+             Predictor_Vars     = predictor_label,
+             Demographic_Vars   = demographic_label,
+             Model_Type         = model_type,
+             R_Squared          = metrics$r_squared,
+             Adjusted_R_Squared = metrics$adj_r_squared,
+             Omega_Squared      = omega_sq,
+             F_Statistic        = metrics$f_statistic,
+             P_Value            = metrics$p_value,
+             N_Observations     = metrics$n_observations,
+             AIC                = metrics$aic,
+             BIC                = metrics$bic,
+             stringsAsFactors   = FALSE
+           ))
+           
+           model_key <- paste(outcome, "|", predictor_label, "|", demographic_label, sep = "")
+           all_regression_models[[model_key]] <- list(
+             model = model_obj, summary = model_summary, metrics = metrics
+           )
+           
+           cat("    ✓ [", demographic_label, "] ω²=", round(omega_sq, 4),
+               " | R²=", round(metrics$r_squared, 4),
+               " | AdjR²=", round(metrics$adj_r_squared, 4), "\n", sep = "")
+         }
+       }
+     }
+   }
+ }
+ 
+ cat("\n\n", rep("=", 70), "\n", sep = "")
+ cat("FINAL RESULTS — sorted by ω²\n")
+ cat(rep("=", 70), "\n\n", sep = "")
+ 
+ results_final <- comprehensive_reg_results[order(-comprehensive_reg_results$Omega_Squared), ]
+ print(results_final[, c("Outcome_Var","Predictor_Vars","Demographic_Vars",
+                          "R_Squared","Adjusted_R_Squared","Omega_Squared","P_Value")])
+ 
+ write.csv(results_final, "comprehensive_regression_results_FINAL.csv", row.names = FALSE, na = "")
+ cat("\n✓ Exported: comprehensive_regression_results_FINAL.csv\n")

+ anova_list <- lapply(categorical_vars, function(cv) {
+   tryCatch({
+     fit <- lm(as.formula(paste("Work_effectively ~", cv)), data = data_SCFs)
+     a   <- anova(fit)
+     eta2 <- a[1, "Sum Sq"] / sum(a[, "Sum Sq"])
+     data.frame(Variable = cv,
+                F_value  = round(a[1, "F value"], 3),
+                p_value  = round(a[1, "Pr(>F)"],  4),
+                Eta2     = round(eta2, 4))
+   }, error = function(e) NULL)
+ })
+ anova_we <- do.call(rbind, anova_list)
+ print(anova_we[order(anova_we$p_value), ])
